3725: function foodStyle(district){

3733: function addFoodIncludedRiGeometry(feature,district,riName,riCode=''){
3734:   const g=feature.geometry;if(!g)return;
3735:   const style=foodStyle(district);
3736:   const displayRi=verifiedRiDisplayName(district,riName,riCode);
3737:   const multi=g.type==='Polygon'?[g.coordinates]:(g.type==='MultiPolygon'?g.coordinates:[]);
3738:   multi.forEach(polyCoords=>{
3739:     if(!polyCoords.length)return;
3740:     const paths=polyCoords.map(ring=>ring.map(([lng,lat])=>new kakao.maps.LatLng(lat,lng)));
3741:     const polygon=new kakao.maps.Polygon({
3742:       path:paths.length===1?paths[0]:paths,
3743:       // 시행지역끼리는 내부 경계를 숨겨 같은 일정 권역이 한 덩어리처럼 보이게 합니다.
3744:       strokeWeight:0,strokeOpacity:0,
3745:       fillColor:style.fill,fillOpacity:.31
3746:     });
3747:     polygon.setMap(activeLayer==='food'?map:null);
3748:     kakao.maps.event.addListener(polygon,'mouseover',(mouseEvent)=>{
3749:       polygon.setOptions({fillOpacity:.45,strokeOpacity:0,strokeWeight:0});
3750:       const e=findRegionEntry('food',district);
3751:       const detail=e&&e.status==='verified'
3752:         ? `음식물 · 분류배출 시행 · ${e.days.join('·')} 배출 · ${e.pickup}`
3753:         : '음식물 · 분류배출 시행지역';
3754:       showZoneTooltip(mouseEvent.latLng,`${district} ${displayRi}`,detail,'법정리 경계 · 분류배출 시행지역');
3755:     });
3756:     kakao.maps.event.addListener(polygon,'mousemove',e=>moveZoneTooltip(e.latLng));
3757:     kakao.maps.event.addListener(polygon,'mouseout',()=>{
3758:       polygon.setOptions({fillOpacity:.31,strokeWeight:0,strokeOpacity:0});
3759:       hideZoneTooltip();
3760:     });
3761:     kakao.maps.event.addListener(polygon,'click',()=>{
3762:       const e=findRegionEntry('food',district);
3763:       const info=e&&e.status==='verified'
3764:         ? `${district} ${displayRi} · 분류배출 시행지역 · ${e.days.join('·')} 배출 · ${e.pickup}`
3765:         : `${district} ${displayRi} · 분류배출 시행지역`;
3766:       updateMapBadge(info);
3767:     });
3768:     zonePolygons.food.push({polygon,district,ri:riName,riCode,displayRi,schedule:style.label,kind:'ri'});
3769:   });
3770: }

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }

1567: function routeItemDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1568:   // 주소 20m 직접매칭은 실제 표시 수거선만 사용합니다.
1569:   let best=Infinity;
1570:   for(const seg of (item?.segments||[])){
1571:     for(let i=1;i<seg.length;i++){
1572:       best=Math.min(best,pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,seg[i-1],seg[i]));
1573:     }
1574:   }
1575:   return best;
1576: }
1577: function serviceZoneEvidenceDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1578:   // ① 수거권역 추정에는 선으로 만들기 어려운 시설·마을명도 '숨은 근거점'으로 활용합니다.
1579:   // 이 점들은 지도에 원/마커로 표시하지 않으며, 주소 20m 직접매칭에도 사용하지 않습니다.

3172:       if(run.estimated){

3176:       }
3177:     }
3178:   }

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }

1566: function routeMatchLimitKm(district){return RURAL_INFERRED_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(canonicalDistrict(district))?RURAL_ROUTE_MATCH_KM:MAX_ADDRESS_ROUTE_DISTANCE_KM} // 주소점에서 실제 표시 수거노선 20m 이내만 수거일정으로 인정
1567: function routeItemDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1568:   // 주소 20m 직접매칭은 실제 표시 수거선만 사용합니다.
1569:   let best=Infinity;
1570:   for(const seg of (item?.segments||[])){
1571:     for(let i=1;i<seg.length;i++){
1572:       best=Math.min(best,pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,seg[i-1],seg[i]));
1573:     }
1574:   }

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }
1565: const MAX_ADDRESS_ROUTE_DISTANCE_KM=0.020;
1566: function routeMatchLimitKm(district){return RURAL_INFERRED_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(canonicalDistrict(district))?RURAL_ROUTE_MATCH_KM:MAX_ADDRESS_ROUTE_DISTANCE_KM} // 주소점에서 실제 표시 수거노선 20m 이내만 수거일정으로 인정
1567: function routeItemDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1568:   // 주소 20m 직접매칭은 실제 표시 수거선만 사용합니다.
1569:   let best=Infinity;
1570:   for(const seg of (item?.segments||[])){
1571:     for(let i=1;i<seg.length;i++){
1572:       best=Math.min(best,pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,seg[i-1],seg[i]));
1573:     }
1574:   }
1575:   return best;
1576: }
1577: function serviceZoneEvidenceDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1578:   // ① 수거권역 추정에는 선으로 만들기 어려운 시설·마을명도 '숨은 근거점'으로 활용합니다.

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }

1566: function routeMatchLimitKm(district){return RURAL_INFERRED_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(canonicalDistrict(district))?RURAL_ROUTE_MATCH_KM:MAX_ADDRESS_ROUTE_DISTANCE_KM} // 주소점에서 실제 표시 수거노선 20m 이내만 수거일정으로 인정
1567: function routeItemDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1568:   // 주소 20m 직접매칭은 실제 표시 수거선만 사용합니다.
1569:   let best=Infinity;
1570:   for(const seg of (item?.segments||[])){
1571:     for(let i=1;i<seg.length;i++){
1572:       best=Math.min(best,pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,seg[i-1],seg[i]));
1573:     }
1574:   }
1575:   return best;
1576: }
1577: function serviceZoneEvidenceDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1578:   // ① 수거권역 추정에는 선으로 만들기 어려운 시설·마을명도 '숨은 근거점'으로 활용합니다.
1579:   // 이 점들은 지도에 원/마커로 표시하지 않으며, 주소 20m 직접매칭에도 사용하지 않습니다.
1580:   let best=routeItemDistanceKm(item,lat,lng);

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }

3733: function addFoodIncludedRiGeometry(feature,district,riName,riCode=''){
3734:   const g=feature.geometry;if(!g)return;
3735:   const style=foodStyle(district);
3736:   const displayRi=verifiedRiDisplayName(district,riName,riCode);
3737:   const multi=g.type==='Polygon'?[g.coordinates]:(g.type==='MultiPolygon'?g.coordinates:[]);
3738:   multi.forEach(polyCoords=>{
3739:     if(!polyCoords.length)return;
3740:     const paths=polyCoords.map(ring=>ring.map(([lng,lat])=>new kakao.maps.LatLng(lat,lng)));
3741:     const polygon=new kakao.maps.Polygon({
3742:       path:paths.length===1?paths[0]:paths,
3743:       // 시행지역끼리는 내부 경계를 숨겨 같은 일정 권역이 한 덩어리처럼 보이게 합니다.
3744:       strokeWeight:0,strokeOpacity:0,
3745:       fillColor:style.fill,fillOpacity:.31
3746:     });
3747:     polygon.setMap(activeLayer==='food'?map:null);
3748:     kakao.maps.event.addListener(polygon,'mouseover',(mouseEvent)=>{
3749:       polygon.setOptions({fillOpacity:.45,strokeOpacity:0,strokeWeight:0});
3750:       const e=findRegionEntry('food',district);
3751:       const detail=e&&e.status==='verified'
3752:         ? `음식물 · 분류배출 시행 · ${e.days.join('·')} 배출 · ${e.pickup}`
3753:         : '음식물 · 분류배출 시행지역';
3754:       showZoneTooltip(mouseEvent.latLng,`${district} ${displayRi}`,detail,'법정리 경계 · 분류배출 시행지역');
3755:     });
3756:     kakao.maps.event.addListener(polygon,'mousemove',e=>moveZoneTooltip(e.latLng));
3757:     kakao.maps.event.addListener(polygon,'mouseout',()=>{
3758:       polygon.setOptions({fillOpacity:.31,strokeWeight:0,strokeOpacity:0});
3759:       hideZoneTooltip();
3760:     });
3761:     kakao.maps.event.addListener(polygon,'click',()=>{
3762:       const e=findRegionEntry('food',district);
3763:       const info=e&&e.status==='verified'
3764:         ? `${district} ${displayRi} · 분류배출 시행지역 · ${e.days.join('·')} 배출 · ${e.pickup}`
3765:         : `${district} ${displayRi} · 분류배출 시행지역`;
3766:       updateMapBadge(info);
3767:     });
3768:     zonePolygons.food.push({polygon,district,ri:riName,riCode,displayRi,schedule:style.label,kind:'ri'});
3769:   });
3770: }

3870: async function fetchGeoJsonAny(urls,label){
3871:   let lastErr=null;
3872:   for(const url of urls){
3873:     try{
3874:       const res=await fetch(url,{cache:'force-cache'});
3875:       if(!res.ok) throw new Error(`${label} HTTP ${res.status}`);
3876:       return await res.json();
3877:     }catch(err){
3878:       lastErr=err;
3879:       console.warn(`${label} 로딩 실패:`,url,err);
3880:     }
3881:   }
3882:   throw lastErr||new Error(`${label} 로딩 실패`);
3883: }

4019:   marker=new kakao.maps.Marker({map,position:new kakao.maps.LatLng(DEFAULT.lat,DEFAULT.lng)});
4020:   geocoder=new kakao.maps.services.Geocoder();places=new kakao.maps.services.Places();
4021:   kakao.maps.event.addListener(map,'click',handleMapClick);
4022:   loadManualZones();
4023:   const bundled=hydrateBundledPrecomputedData();
4024:   if(!bundled){hydrateCachedServiceZones('general').then(()=>setLayer('general'));hydrateCachedServiceZones('recycle');}
4025:   setLayer('general');
4026:   // 내장 좌표는 즉시 렌더링하고, 행정경계/누락분 점검은 뒤에서 진행합니다.

4028:   zoneBuildPromise=buildZonePolygons();

4030: }
4031: function loadSdk(){const s=document.createElement('script');s.src=`https://dapi.kakao.com/v2/maps/sdk.js?appkey=${KAKAO_KEY}&libraries=services&autoload=false`;s.onload=()=>kakao.maps.load(init);s.onerror=()=>setLandingStatus('카카오맵 API를 불러오지 못했습니다. 등록 도메인을 확인해주세요.',true);document.head.appendChild(s)}
